                  @for (
                    agent of agents();

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      (click)="selectAgent(agent)"
                      class="w-full rounded-xl border p-4 text-left transition"

                        form.controls.agentIdentityUserId
                          .value === agent.id
                      "

                          .value === agent.id
                      "

                        form.controls.agentIdentityUserId

                        form.controls.agentIdentityUserId
                          .value !== agent.id
                      "
                    >
                      <div
                        class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4"
                      >
                        <div class="min-w-0">
                          <p
                            class="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                          >
                            {{ agent.firstName }}
                            {{ agent.lastName }}
                          </p>

                          <p
                            class="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-zinc-500"
                          >
                            {{ agent.email }}
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        <span
                          class="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border"
                          [class.border-[var(--color-primary)]]="
                            form.controls
                              .agentIdentityUserId
                              .value === agent.id
                          "
                          [class.border-zinc-300]="
                            form.controls
                              .agentIdentityUserId
                              .value !== agent.id
                          "
                        >
                          @if (
                            form.controls
                              .agentIdentityUserId
                              .value === agent.id
                          ) {
                            <span
                              class="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--color-primary)]"
                              aria-hidden="true"
                            ></span>
                          }
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  }
                </div>

                @if (
                  form.controls.agentIdentityUserId.touched &&
                  form.controls.agentIdentityUserId.invalid
                ) {
                  <p
                    class="mt-3 text-sm text-[var(--color-danger)]"
                  >
                    Select an Agent before continuing.
                  </p>
                }
              </form>

              <!-- Assignment warning -->
              <div
                class="mt-7 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5"
              >
                <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div
                    class="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-amber-100 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-warning)]"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  >
                    !
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <p
                      class="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                    >
                      Permanent assignment
                    </p>

                    <p
                      class="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-600"
                    >
                      After confirmation, this Customer
                      cannot be transferred to another Agent
                      or returned to the unassigned queue.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <!-- Selected Agent summary -->
              @if (selectedAgent(); as selected) {
                <div
                  class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50 p-4"
                >
                  <p
                    class="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-[var(--color-primary)]"
                  >
                    Selected Agent
                  </p>

                  <p
                    class="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"

                    {{ selected.firstName }}
                    {{ selected.lastName }}

                  <p
                    class="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500"
                  >

              <!-- Assignment button -->
              <button
                type="button"

                [disabled]="
                  form.invalid ||

                  !!customerErrorMessage() ||

                class="mt-7 flex w-full items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[var(--color-primary)] px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[var(--color-primary-dark)] focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-200 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                @if (isAssigning()) {
                  Assigning Agent...
                } @else {
                  Assign Agent
                }
              </button>
            }
          </section>
        }
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ================================================= -->
  <!-- CONFIRMATION DIALOG -->
  <!-- ================================================= -->

  @if (
    showConfirmation() &&
    selectedAgent();
    as agent
  ) {
    <div
      class="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/45 px-5"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="assignment-title"
    >
      <section
        class="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl sm:p-7"
      >
        <div
          class="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-100 font-semibold text-[var(--color-warning)]"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          !
        </div>

        <p
          class="mt-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--color-warning)]"
        >
          Permanent assignment
        </p>

        <h2
          id="assignment-title"

        <p
          class="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-600"
        >
          This Customer will be permanently assigned to:
        </p>

        <!-- Selected Agent -->
        <div
          class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] bg-[var(--color-warm-white)] p-4"
        >
          <p
            class="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
          >
            {{ agent.firstName }} {{ agent.lastName }}
          </p>

          <p
            class="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500"
          >
            {{ agent.email }}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div
          class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4"
        >
          <p
            class="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
          >
            This action cannot be undone
          </p>

          <p
            class="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-600"
          >
            The assignment cannot be reassigned,
            transferred, removed, or returned to the
            unassigned queue in the current workflow.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div
          class="mt-7 flex justify-end gap-3"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            (click)="cancelConfirmation()"

          >
            Cancel

          <button
            type="button"

            class="rounded-xl bg-[var(--color-primary)] px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[var(--color-primary-dark)] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            @if (isAssigning()) {
              Assigning...
            } @else {
              Confirm Assignment
            }
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>

FILE: customer-vehicle\pages\admin-assignment-review\admin-assignment-review.ts
================================================================================
import {
  Component,
  OnInit,
  inject,
  signal
} from '@angular/core';
import { DatePipe } from '@angular/common';
import {
  FormBuilder,
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';
import {
  ActivatedRoute,
  Router
} from '@angular/router';
import { finalize } from 'rxjs';

import {
  AssignmentReviewCustomer,
  EligibleAgent
} from '../../../../core/models/agent-assignment.models';
import {
  CustomerVehicleApi
} from '../../services/customer-vehicle-api';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-admin-assignment-review',
  imports: [
    DatePipe,
    ReactiveFormsModule
  ],
  templateUrl: './admin-assignment-review.html',
  styleUrl: './admin-assignment-review.css'
})
export class AdminAssignmentReview implements OnInit {
  private readonly formBuilder =
    inject(FormBuilder);

  private readonly route =
    inject(ActivatedRoute);

  private readonly router =
    inject(Router);

  private readonly customerVehicleApi =
    inject(CustomerVehicleApi);

  readonly customer =
    signal<AssignmentReviewCustomer | null>(
      null
    );

  readonly agents =
    signal<EligibleAgent[]>([]);

  readonly isLoadingCustomer =
    signal(true);

  readonly isLoadingAgents =
    signal(true);

  readonly isAssigning =
    signal(false);

  readonly customerErrorMessage =
    signal<string | null>(null);

  readonly agentsErrorMessage =
    signal<string | null>(null);

  readonly assignmentErrorMessage =
    signal<string | null>(null);

  readonly showConfirmation =
    signal(false);

  readonly successMessage =
    signal<string | null>(null);

  readonly form =
    this.formBuilder.nonNullable.group({
      agentIdentityUserId: [
        '',
        Validators.required
      ]
    });

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const customerProfileId =
      this.getCustomerProfileId();

    if (!customerProfileId) {
      this.back();
      return;
    }

    this.loadCustomer(
      customerProfileId
    );

    this.loadEligibleAgents();
  }

  selectAgent(
    agent: EligibleAgent
  ): void {
    this.form.controls.agentIdentityUserId
      .setValue(agent.id);

    this.assignmentErrorMessage.set(null);
  }

  selectedAgent():
    EligibleAgent | undefined {
    const selectedId =
      this.form.controls
        .agentIdentityUserId.value;

    return this.agents().find(
      agent =>
        agent.id === selectedId
    );
  }

  openConfirmation(): void {
    if (
      this.form.invalid ||
      this.isAssigning()
    ) {
      this.form.markAllAsTouched();
      return;
    }

    this.assignmentErrorMessage.set(null);

    this.showConfirmation.set(true);
  }

  cancelConfirmation(): void {
    this.showConfirmation.set(false);
  }

  confirmAssignment(): void {
    const customer =
      this.customer();

    if (
      !customer ||
      this.form.invalid ||
      this.isAssigning()
    ) {
      return;
    }

    this.showConfirmation.set(false);

    this.assignmentErrorMessage.set(null);

    this.isAssigning.set(true);

    this.customerVehicleApi
      .assignAgent(
        customer.customerProfileId,
        {
          agentIdentityUserId:
            this.form.controls
              .agentIdentityUserId
              .value
        }
      )
      .pipe(
        finalize(() => {
          this.isAssigning.set(false);
        })
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.successMessage.set(
            'Agent assigned successfully.'
          );

          setTimeout(() => {
            void this.router.navigate([
              '/admin/customer-assignments'
            ]);
          }, 800);
        },

        error: error => {
          if (error?.status === 409) {
            this.assignmentErrorMessage.set(
              error?.error?.message ??
              'The assignment could not be completed because the customer or Agent state changed.'
            );

            return;
          }

          if (error?.status === 503) {
            this.assignmentErrorMessage.set(
              'Identity service is currently unavailable. No assignment was created. Please try again.'
            );

            return;
          }

          if (error?.status === 404) {
            this.assignmentErrorMessage.set(
              'The customer could not be found.'
            );

            return;
          }

          this.assignmentErrorMessage.set(
            'Agent assignment could not be completed. Please try again.'
          );
        }
      });
  }

  retryCustomer(): void {
    const customerProfileId =
      this.getCustomerProfileId();

    if (!customerProfileId) {
      this.back();
      return;
    }

    this.loadCustomer(
      customerProfileId
    );
  }

  retryAgents(): void {
    this.loadEligibleAgents();
  }

  back(): void {
    void this.router.navigate([
      '/admin/customer-assignments'
    ]);
  }

  private getCustomerProfileId():
    string | null {
    return this.route.snapshot
      .paramMap
      .get('id');
  }

  private loadCustomer(
    customerProfileId: string
  ): void {
    this.customerErrorMessage.set(null);
    this.isLoadingCustomer.set(true);

    this.customerVehicleApi
      .getCustomerForAssignment(
        customerProfileId
      )
      .pipe(
        finalize(() => {
          this.isLoadingCustomer.set(
            false
          );
        })
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: customer => {
          if (customer.isAssigned) {
            this.customer.set(null);

            this.customerErrorMessage.set(
              'This customer already has a permanent Agent assignment.'
            );

            return;
          }

          this.customer.set(customer);
        },

        error: error => {
          this.customer.set(null);

          if (error?.status === 404) {
            this.customerErrorMessage.set(
              'The customer could not be found.'
            );

            return;
          }

          if (error?.status === 403) {
            this.customerErrorMessage.set(
              'You are not authorized to review this customer.'
            );

            return;
          }

          this.customerErrorMessage.set(
            'Customer information could not be loaded. Please try again.'
          );
        }
      });
  }

  private loadEligibleAgents(): void {
    this.agentsErrorMessage.set(null);
    this.isLoadingAgents.set(true);

    this.customerVehicleApi
      .getEligibleAgents()
      .pipe(
        finalize(() => {
          this.isLoadingAgents.set(
            false
          );
        })
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: agents => {
          this.agents.set(agents);

          const selectedId =
            this.form.controls
              .agentIdentityUserId.value;

          if (
            selectedId &&
            !agents.some(
              agent =>
                agent.id === selectedId
            )
          ) {
            this.form.controls
              .agentIdentityUserId
              .reset();
          }
        },

        error: error => {
          this.agents.set([]);

          if (error?.status === 503) {
            this.agentsErrorMessage.set(
              'Identity service is currently unavailable. Eligible Agents could not be loaded.'
            );

            return;
          }

          if (error?.status === 404) {
            this.agentsErrorMessage.set(
              'The eligible Agent endpoint could not be found.'
            );

            return;
          }

          if (error?.status === 403) {
            this.agentsErrorMessage.set(
              'You are not authorized to view eligible Agents.'
            );

            return;
          }

          this.agentsErrorMessage.set(
            'Eligible Agents could not be loaded. Please try again.'
          );
        }
      });
  }
}

FILE: customer-vehicle\pages\admin-unassigned-customers\admin-unassigned-customers.ts
================================================================================
import {
  Component,
  OnInit,
  inject,
  signal
} from '@angular/core';
import { DatePipe } from '@angular/common';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { finalize } from 'rxjs';

import {
  UnassignedCustomer
} from '../../../../core/models/agent-assignment.models';
import {
  CustomerVehicleApi
} from '../../services/customer-vehicle-api';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-admin-unassigned-customers',
  imports: [
    DatePipe
  ],
  templateUrl: './admin-unassigned-customers.html',
  styleUrl: './admin-unassigned-customers.css'
})
export class AdminUnassignedCustomers
  implements OnInit {
  private readonly customerVehicleApi =
    inject(CustomerVehicleApi);

  private readonly router =
    inject(Router);

  readonly customers =
    signal<UnassignedCustomer[]>([]);

  readonly isLoading =
    signal(true);

  readonly errorMessage =
    signal<string | null>(null);

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.loadCustomers();
  }

review(
  customer: UnassignedCustomer
): void {
  void this.router.navigate([
    '/admin/customer-assignments',
    customer.customerProfileId
  ]);
}

  retry(): void {
    this.loadCustomers();
  }

  private loadCustomers(): void {
    this.errorMessage.set(null);
    this.isLoading.set(true);

    this.customerVehicleApi
      .getUnassignedCustomers()
      .pipe(
        finalize(() => {
          this.isLoading.set(false);
        })
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: customers => {
          this.customers.set(customers);
        },

        error: error => {
          if (error?.status === 503) {
            this.errorMessage.set(
              'Customer information is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.'
            );

            return;
          }

          this.errorMessage.set(
            'Unassigned customers could not be loaded. Please try again.'
          );
        }
      });
  }
}

            class="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-600"
          >
            Add Vehicle value, usage and ownership information.
          </p>


          <form
            [formGroup]="form"
            class="mt-7"
          >

            <div
              class="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2"
            >

              <!-- Value -->

              <div>

                <label
                  for="vehicleValue"
                  class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  Vehicle Value
                </label>


                <input
                  id="vehicleValue"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  formControlName="vehicleValue"
                  class="w-full rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs outline-none focus:border-[var(--color-primary)] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />

              </div>


              <!-- Odometer -->

              <div>

                <label
                  for="currentOdometer"
                  class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  Current Odometer
                </label>


                <input
                  id="currentOdometer"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  formControlName="currentOdometer"
                  class="w-full rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs outline-none focus:border-[var(--color-primary)] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />

              </div>


              <!-- Mileage -->

              <div>

                <label
                  for="annualMileage"
                  class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  Annual Mileage
                </label>


                <input
                  id="annualMileage"
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  formControlName="annualMileage"
                  class="w-full rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs outline-none focus:border-[var(--color-primary)] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />

              </div>


              <!-- Purchase Date -->

              <div>

                <label
                  for="purchaseDate"
                  class="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  Purchase Date
                </label>


                <input
                  id="purchaseDate"
                  type="date"
                  formControlName="purchaseDate"
                  class="w-full rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs outline-none focus:border-[var(--color-primary)] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />

              </div>

            </div>


            <!-- Ownership -->

            <div
              class="mt-7"
            >

              <p
                class="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
              >
                Ownership Type
              </p>


              <div
                class="mt-3 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2"
              >

                <!-- Owned -->

                <label
                  class="cursor-pointer rounded-xl border p-4 transition"
                  [class.border-[var(--color-primary)]]="
                    form.controls.ownershipType.value === '1'
                  "
                  [class.bg-blue-50]="
                    form.controls.ownershipType.value === '1'
                  "
                  [class.border-[var(--color-border)]]="
                    form.controls.ownershipType.value !== '1'
                  "
                >

                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    formControlName="ownershipType"
                    value="1"
                    class="sr-only"
                  />


                  <p
                    class="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    Owned
                  </p>


                  <p
                    class="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-500"
                  >
                    Vehicle is owned outright.
                  </p>

                </label>


                <!-- Financed -->

                <label
                  class="cursor-pointer rounded-xl border p-4 transition"
                  [class.border-[var(--color-primary)]]="
                    form.controls.ownershipType.value === '2'
                  "
                  [class.bg-blue-50]="
                    form.controls.ownershipType.value === '2'
                  "
                  [class.border-[var(--color-border)]]="
                    form.controls.ownershipType.value !== '2'
                  "
                >

                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    formControlName="ownershipType"
                    value="2"
                    class="sr-only"
                  />


                  <p
                    class="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    Financed
                  </p>


                  <p
                    class="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-500"
                  >
                    Vehicle is under active financing.
                  </p>

                </label>

              </div>

            </div>

          </form>

        </div>

      }


      <!-- ================================================= -->
      <!-- STEP 3 -->
      <!-- ================================================= -->

      @if (
        currentStep() === 3
      ) {

        <div>

          <p
            class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--color-primary)]"
          >
            Step 3 of 4
          </p>


          <h2
            class="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.025em] text-[var(--color-ink)] sm:text-2xl"
          >
            Vehicle Image
          </h2>


          <p
            class="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-600"
          >
            Add an optional Vehicle image. Images do not affect
            Vehicle readiness.
          </p>


          <div
            class="mt-7 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 bg-zinc-50 p-6"
          >

            @if (
              imagePreviewUrl();
              as preview
            ) {

              <div>

                <img
                  [src]="preview"
                  alt="Selected Vehicle preview"
                  class="mx-auto max-h-[360px] w-full rounded-xl object-contain"
                />


                @if (
                  selectedImage();
                  as image
                ) {

                  <div
                    class="mt-5 text-center"
                  >

                    <p
                      class="break-all text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                    >
                      {{ image.name }}
                    </p>


                    <p
                      class="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500"
                    >
                      {{ (image.size / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2) }}
                      MB
                    </p>

                  </div>

                }


                <div
                  class="mt-5 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3"
                >

                  <label
                    for="vehicleImageReplace"
                    class="cursor-pointer rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--color-primary)] transition hover:bg-blue-50"
                  >
                    Replace Image
                  </label>


                  <input
                    id="vehicleImageReplace"
                    type="file"
                    accept=".jpg,.jpeg,.png,image/jpeg,image/png"
                    class="hidden"
                    (change)="onImageSelected($event)"
                  />


                  <button
                    type="button"
                    (click)="removeImage()"
                    class="rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 transition hover:bg-zinc-50"
                  >
                    Remove
                  </button>

                </div>

              </div>

            } @else {

              <div
                class="py-8 text-center"
              >

                <div
                  class="mx-auto flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--color-primary)] shadow-sm"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                >
                  +
                </div>


                <h3
                  class="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  Add Vehicle image
                </h3>


                <p
                  class="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-500"
                >
                  JPG, JPEG or PNG. Maximum 5 MB.
                </p>


                <label
                  for="vehicleImage"
                  class="mt-6 inline-flex cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-[var(--color-primary)] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[var(--color-primary-dark)]"
                >
                  Choose Image
                </label>


                <input
                  id="vehicleImage"
                  type="file"
                  accept=".jpg,.jpeg,.png,image/jpeg,image/png"
                  class="hidden"
                  (change)="onImageSelected($event)"
                />


                <p
                  class="mt-4 text-xs text-zinc-400"
                >
                  You can continue without an image.
                </p>

              </div>

            }

          </div>


          @if (
            imageErrorMessage()
          ) {

            <div
              class="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4"
              role="alert"
            >

              <p
                class="text-sm leading-5 text-[var(--color-danger)]"
              >
                {{ imageErrorMessage() }}
              </p>


              @if (
                vehicleCreated()
              ) {

                <button
                  type="button"
                  (click)="continueWithoutImage()"
                  class="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--color-danger)]"
                >
                  Continue Without Image
                </button>

              }

            </div>

          }

        </div>

      }


      <!-- ================================================= -->
      <!-- STEP 4 -->
      <!-- ================================================= -->

      @if (
        currentStep() === 4
      ) {

        <div>

          <p
            class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--color-primary)]"
          >
            Step 4 of 4
          </p>


          <h2
            class="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.025em] text-[var(--color-ink)] sm:text-2xl"
          >
            Review Vehicle
          </h2>


          <p
            class="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-600"
          >
            Review the Vehicle information before saving.
          </p>


          <div
            class="mt-7 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2"
          >

            <!-- Vehicle Details -->

            <article
              class="rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] p-5"
            >

              <p
                class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-zinc-400"
              >
                Vehicle Details
              </p>


              <h3
                class="mt-3 text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
              >
                {{ form.controls.make.value }}
                {{ form.controls.model.value }}
              </h3>


              <dl
                class="mt-5 space-y-3"
              >

                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    VIN
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="break-all text-right font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{ form.controls.vin.value }}
                  </dd>

                </div>


                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Registration
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="text-right font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{
                      form.controls
                        .registrationNumber
                        .value
                    }}
                  </dd>

                </div>


                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Year
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{
                      form.controls
                        .manufacturingYear
                        .value
                    }}
                  </dd>

                </div>


                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Type
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{ vehicleTypeLabel() }}
                  </dd>

                </div>


                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Fuel
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{ fuelTypeLabel() }}
                  </dd>

                </div>

              </dl>

            </article>


            <!-- Ownership -->

            <article
              class="rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] p-5"
            >

              <p
                class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-zinc-400"
              >
                Ownership & Usage
              </p>


              <dl
                class="mt-5 space-y-3"
              >

                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Vehicle Value
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{ form.controls.vehicleValue.value }}
                  </dd>

                </div>


                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Odometer
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{ form.controls.currentOdometer.value }}
                  </dd>

                </div>


                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Annual Mileage
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{ form.controls.annualMileage.value }}
                  </dd>

                </div>


                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Ownership
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{ ownershipTypeLabel() }}
                  </dd>

                </div>


                <div
                  class="flex items-start justify-between gap-5 text-xs"
                >

                  <dt class="text-zinc-500">
                    Purchase Date
                  </dt>

                  <dd
                    class="font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                  >
                    {{
                      form.controls
                        .purchaseDate
                        .value
                    }}
                  </dd>

                </div>

              </dl>

            </article>


            <!-- Image -->

            <article
              class="rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] p-5 lg:col-span-2"
            >

              <p
                class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-zinc-400"
              >
                Vehicle Image
              </p>


              @if (
                imagePreviewUrl();
                as preview
              ) {

                <div
                  class="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
                >

                  <img
                    [src]="preview"
                    alt="Vehicle preview"
                    class="h-28 w-44 rounded-xl object-cover"
                  />


                  @if (
                    selectedImage();
                    as image
                  ) {

                    <div>

                      <p
                        class="break-all text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                      >
                        {{ image.name }}
                      </p>


                      <p
                        class="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-500"
                      >
                        Optional image will be uploaded after
                        Vehicle creation.
                      </p>

                    </div>

                  }

                </div>

              } @else {

                <p
                  class="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-zinc-500"
                >
                  No image selected. The Vehicle can be saved
                  without an image.
                </p>

              }

            </article>

          </div>

        </div>

      }


      <!-- ================================================= -->
      <!-- WORKFLOW ACTIONS -->
      <!-- ================================================= -->

      <div
        class="mt-8 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-[var(--color-border)] pt-6"
      >

        <button
          type="button"
          (click)="previous()"
          [disabled]="
            currentStep() === 1 ||
            isSaving()
          "
          class="rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] bg-white px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 transition hover:bg-zinc-50 disabled:invisible"
        >
          Back
        </button>


        @if (
          currentStep() < 4
        ) {

          <button
            type="button"
            (click)="next()"
            [disabled]="isSaving()"
            class="rounded-xl bg-[var(--color-primary)] px-6 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[var(--color-primary-dark)] disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            Continue
          </button>

        } @else {

          <button
            type="button"
            (click)="saveVehicle()"
            [disabled]="
              form.invalid ||
              isSaving() ||
              vehicleCreated()
            "
            class="rounded-xl bg-[var(--color-primary)] px-6 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[var(--color-primary-dark)] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          >

            @if (
              isSaving()
            ) {
              Saving Vehicle...
            } @else {
              Save Vehicle
            }

          </button>

        }

      </div>

    </section>

  </div>

</main>



================================================================================
FILE: customer-vehicle\pages\agent-add-vehicle\agent-add-vehicle.ts
================================================================================
import {
  ChangeDetectionStrategy,
  Component,
  OnDestroy,
  OnInit,
  inject,
  signal
} from '@angular/core';

import {
  FormBuilder,
  ReactiveFormsModule,
  Validators
} from '@angular/forms';

import {
  ActivatedRoute,
  Router
} from '@angular/router';

import {
  finalize
} from 'rxjs';

import {
  FuelType,
  OwnershipType,
  UpsertVehicleRequest,
  Vehicle,
  VehicleType
} from '../../../../core/models/vehicle.models';

import {
  CustomerVehicleApi
} from '../../services/customer-vehicle-api';


type VehicleStep =
  | 1
  | 2
  | 3
  | 4;


@Component({
  selector: 'app-agent-add-vehicle',
  imports: [
    ReactiveFormsModule
  ],
  templateUrl: './agent-add-vehicle.html',
  styleUrl: './agent-add-vehicle.css',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush
})
export class AgentAddVehicle
  implements OnInit, OnDestroy {

  private readonly formBuilder =
    inject(FormBuilder);

  private readonly route =
    inject(ActivatedRoute);

  private readonly router =
    inject(Router);

  private readonly customerVehicleApi =
    inject(CustomerVehicleApi);


  private customerProfileId:
    string | null = null;

  private createdVehicle:
    Vehicle | null = null;


  /* =====================================================
   * WORKFLOW
   * ===================================================== */

  readonly currentStep =
    signal<VehicleStep>(1);


  /* =====================================================
   * IMAGE
   * ===================================================== */

  readonly selectedImage =
    signal<File | null>(null);

  readonly imagePreviewUrl =
    signal<string | null>(null);

  readonly imageErrorMessage =
    signal<string | null>(null);


  /* =====================================================
   * GENERAL STATE
   * ===================================================== */

  readonly errorMessage =
    signal<string | null>(null);

  readonly isSaving =
    signal(false);

  readonly vehicleCreated =
    signal(false);


  /* =====================================================
   * FORM
   * ===================================================== */

  readonly form =
    this.formBuilder.nonNullable.group({

      vin: [
        '',
        [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.minLength(17),
          Validators.maxLength(17)
        ]
      ],

      registrationNumber: [
        '',
        [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.maxLength(50)
        ]
      ],

      make: [
        '',
        [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.maxLength(100)
        ]
      ],

      model: [
        '',
        [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.maxLength(100)
        ]
      ],

      manufacturingYear: [
        null as number | null,
        [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.min(1900)
        ]
      ],

      vehicleType: [
        '',
        Validators.required
      ],

      fuelType: [
        '',
        Validators.required
      ],

      vehicleValue: [
        null as number | null,
        [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.min(0.01)
        ]
      ],

      currentOdometer: [
        null as number | null,
        [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.min(0)
        ]
      ],

      annualMileage: [
        null as number | null,
        [
          Validators.required,
          Validators.min(1)
        ]
      ],

      ownershipType: [
        '',
        Validators.required
      ],

      purchaseDate: [
        '',
        Validators.required
      ]

    });


  /* =====================================================
   * LIFECYCLE
   * ===================================================== */

  ngOnInit(): void {

    this.customerProfileId =
      this.route.snapshot
        .paramMap
        .get('customerId');


    if (!this.customerProfileId) {

      void this.router.navigate([
        '/agent/customers'
      ]);
    }
  }


  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.revokeImagePreview();
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * STEP NAVIGATION
   * ===================================================== */

  next(): void {

    this.errorMessage.set(null);


    if (
      this.currentStep() === 1
    ) {

      if (
        !this.validateVehicleDetails()
      ) {
        return;
      }


      this.currentStep.set(2);

      return;
    }


    if (
      this.currentStep() === 2
    ) {

      if (
        !this.validateOwnershipAndUsage()
      ) {
        return;
      }


      this.currentStep.set(3);

      return;
    }


    if (
      this.currentStep() === 3
    ) {

      this.currentStep.set(4);
    }
  }


  previous(): void {

    if (
      this.currentStep() <= 1 ||
      this.isSaving()
    ) {
      return;
    }


    this.errorMessage.set(null);


    this.currentStep.update(
      step =>
        (step - 1) as VehicleStep
    );
  }


  goToStep(
    step: VehicleStep
  ): void {

    if (
      step <
      this.currentStep() &&
      !this.isSaving()
    ) {

      this.currentStep.set(
        step
      );
    }
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * IMAGE SELECTION
   * ===================================================== */

  onImageSelected(
    event: Event
  ): void {

    const input =
      event.target as HTMLInputElement;


    const file =
      input.files?.[0];


    if (!file) {
      return;
    }


    this.imageErrorMessage.set(
      null
    );


    const allowedTypes = [
      'image/jpeg',
      'image/png'
    ];


    if (
      !allowedTypes.includes(
        file.type
      )
    ) {

      input.value = '';


      this.imageErrorMessage.set(
        'Vehicle image must be JPG, JPEG, or PNG.'
      );

      return;
    }


    const maximumSize =
      5 * 1024 * 1024;


    if (
      file.size >
      maximumSize
    ) {

      input.value = '';


      this.imageErrorMessage.set(
        'Vehicle image cannot exceed 5 MB.'
      );

      return;
    }


    this.revokeImagePreview();


    this.selectedImage.set(
      file
    );


    this.imagePreviewUrl.set(
      URL.createObjectURL(file)
    );
  }


  removeImage(): void {

    this.revokeImagePreview();

    this.selectedImage.set(
      null
    );

    this.imageErrorMessage.set(
      null
    );
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * SAVE VEHICLE
   * ===================================================== */

  saveVehicle(): void {

    if (
      !this.customerProfileId ||
      this.form.invalid ||
      this.isSaving()
    ) {

      this.form.markAllAsTouched();

      return;
    }


    const values =
      this.form.getRawValue();


    const request:
      UpsertVehicleRequest = {

        vin:
          values.vin
            .trim()
            .toUpperCase(),

        registrationNumber:
          values.registrationNumber
            .trim()
            .toUpperCase(),

        make:
          values.make
            .trim(),

        model:
          values.model
            .trim(),

        manufacturingYear:
          values.manufacturingYear!,

        vehicleType:
          Number(
            values.vehicleType
          ) as VehicleType,

        fuelType:
          Number(
            values.fuelType
          ) as FuelType,

        vehicleValue:
          values.vehicleValue!,

        currentOdometer:
          values.currentOdometer!,

        annualMileage:
          values.annualMileage!,

        ownershipType:
          Number(
            values.ownershipType
          ) as OwnershipType,

        purchaseDate:
          values.purchaseDate

      };


    this.errorMessage.set(null);

    this.imageErrorMessage.set(null);

    this.isSaving.set(true);


    this.customerVehicleApi
      .createAgentVehicle(
        this.customerProfileId,
        request
      )
      .subscribe({

        next: vehicle => {

          this.createdVehicle =
            vehicle;


          this.vehicleCreated.set(
            true
          );


          const image =
            this.selectedImage();


          if (!image) {

            this.finish(
              vehicle.id
            );

            return;
          }


          this.uploadImage(
            vehicle.id,
            image
          );
        },


        error: error => {

          this.isSaving.set(
            false
          );


          if (
            error?.status === 409
          ) {

            this.errorMessage.set(
              error?.error?.message ??
              'VIN or Registration Number is already in use.'
            );

            return;
          }


          if (
            error?.status === 403
          ) {

            this.errorMessage.set(
              'This customer is not assigned to your Agent account.'
            );

            return;
          }


          if (
            error?.status === 404
          ) {

            this.errorMessage.set(
              'The customer could not be found.'
            );

            return;
          }


          if (
            error?.status === 400
          ) {

            this.errorMessage.set(
              error?.error?.message ??
              'Please review the Vehicle information.'
            );

            return;
          }


          this.errorMessage.set(
            'Vehicle could not be created. Please try again.'
          );
        }

      });
  }


  continueWithoutImage(): void {

    const vehicle =
      this.createdVehicle;


    if (!vehicle) {
      return;
    }


    this.finish(
      vehicle.id
    );
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * CANCEL
   * ===================================================== */

  cancel(): void {

    if (!this.customerProfileId) {

      void this.router.navigate([
        '/agent/customers'
      ]);

      return;
    }


    void this.router.navigate([
      '/agent/customers',
      this.customerProfileId
    ]);
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * DISPLAY HELPERS
   * ===================================================== */

  vehicleTypeLabel(): string {

    const value =
      Number(
        this.form.controls
          .vehicleType.value
      );


    const labels:
      Record<number, string> = {

        1: 'Sedan',

        2: 'Hatchback',

        3: 'SUV',

        4: 'Coupe',

        5: 'Convertible',

        6: 'Pickup',

        7: 'Van',

        8: 'Other'

      };


    return labels[value] ??
      'Not selected';
  }


  fuelTypeLabel(): string {

    const value =
      Number(
        this.form.controls
          .fuelType.value
      );


    const labels:
      Record<number, string> = {

        1: 'Petrol',

        2: 'Diesel',

        3: 'Electric',

        4: 'Hybrid',

        5: 'Other'

      };


    return labels[value] ??
      'Not selected';
  }


  ownershipTypeLabel(): string {

    return Number(
      this.form.controls
        .ownershipType.value
    ) === 2
      ? 'Financed'
      : 'Owned';
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * IMAGE UPLOAD AFTER VEHICLE CREATION
   * ===================================================== */

  private uploadImage(
    vehicleId: string,
    file: File
  ): void {

    this.customerVehicleApi
      .uploadVehicleImage(
        vehicleId,
        file
      )
      .pipe(
        finalize(() => {

          this.isSaving.set(
            false
          );

        })
      )
      .subscribe({

        next: () => {

          this.finish(
            vehicleId
          );
        },


        error: error => {

          /*
           * Vehicle creation succeeded.
           *
           * Do not create the Vehicle again just because
           * the optional image upload failed.
           */

          this.imageErrorMessage.set(
            error?.error?.message ??
            'Vehicle was created, but the image could not be uploaded. You can continue without the image.'
          );
        }

      });
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * FINISH
   * ===================================================== */

  private finish(
    vehicleId: string
  ): void {

    this.isSaving.set(
      false
    );


    void this.router.navigate([
      '/agent/customers',
      this.customerProfileId,
      'vehicles',
      vehicleId
    ]);
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * VALIDATION
   * ===================================================== */

  private validateVehicleDetails():
    boolean {

    const controls = [

      this.form.controls.vin,

      this.form.controls
        .registrationNumber,

      this.form.controls.make,

      this.form.controls.model,

      this.form.controls
        .manufacturingYear,

      this.form.controls
        .vehicleType,

      this.form.controls
        .fuelType

    ];


    controls.forEach(
      control =>
        control.markAsTouched()
    );


    return controls.every(
      control =>
        control.valid
    );
  }


  private validateOwnershipAndUsage():
    boolean {

    const controls = [

      this.form.controls
        .vehicleValue,

      this.form.controls
        .currentOdometer,

      this.form.controls
        .annualMileage,

      this.form.controls
        .ownershipType,

      this.form.controls
        .purchaseDate

    ];


    controls.forEach(
      control =>
        control.markAsTouched()
    );


    return controls.every(
      control =>
        control.valid
    );
  }


  /* =====================================================
   * CLEANUP
   * ===================================================== */

  private revokeImagePreview(): void {

    const currentUrl =
      this.imagePreviewUrl();


    if (currentUrl) {

      URL.revokeObjectURL(
        currentUrl
      );
    }


    this.imagePreviewUrl.set(
      null
    );
  }
}

                @if (
                  driverForm.controls.licenceExpiryDate.touched &&
                  driverForm.controls.licenceExpiryDate.invalid
                ) {

                  <p
                    class="mt-1.5 text-[10px] text-[var(--color-danger)]"
                  >
                    Licence Expiry Date is required.
                  </p>
                }
              </div>

            </div>


            <div
              class="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse justify-end gap-3 border-t border-[var(--color-border)] pt-5 sm:flex-row"
            >

              <button
                type="button"
                (click)="cancelDriverForm()"
                [disabled]="isSavingDriver()"
                class="rounded-xl border border-[var(--color-border)] bg-white px-5 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 transition hover:bg-zinc-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>


              <button
                type="submit"
                [disabled]="isSavingDriver()"
                class="rounded-xl bg-[var(--color-primary)] px-5 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[var(--color-primary-dark)] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                @if (
                  isSavingDriver()
                ) {
                  Saving Driver...
                } @else {
                  Save Driver
                }
              </button>

            </div>

          </form>
        }


        <!-- Driver details -->

        @if (
          !isLoadingDriver() &&
          !isEditingDriver() &&
          driver();
          as currentDriver
        ) {

          <section
            class="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--color-border)] bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6"
          >

            <div
              class="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2"
            >

              <div>
                <p
                  class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-zinc-400"
                >
                  Licence Number
                </p>

                <p
                  class="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  {{ currentDriver.licenceNumber }}
                </p>
              </div>


              <div>
                <p
                  class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-zinc-400"
                >
                  Licence State
                </p>

                <p
                  class="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  {{ currentDriver.licenceState }}
                </p>
              </div>


              <div>
                <p
                  class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-zinc-400"
                >
                  Issue Date
                </p>

                <p
                  class="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  {{
                    currentDriver.licenceIssueDate
                      | date: 'dd MMM yyyy'
                  }}
                </p>
              </div>


              <div>
                <p
                  class="text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-zinc-400"
                >
                  Expiry Date
                </p>

                <p
                  class="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                >
                  {{
                    currentDriver.licenceExpiryDate
                      | date: 'dd MMM yyyy'
                  }}
                </p>
              </div>

            </div>

          </section>
        }


        <!-- No driver -->

        @if (
          !isLoadingDriver() &&
          !isEditingDriver() &&
          !driver() &&
          !driverErrorMessage()
        ) {

          <section
            class="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5"
          >

            <p
              class="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-800"
            >
              No Primary Driver recorded

    <!-- ======================================================= -->

        <!-- Vehicle error -->

        ) {

                    aria-hidden="true"

                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >

                      </div>

                        No image
                      </p>

                    </div>
                  }

                </div>


                <div
                  class="p-5"
                >

                  <div
                    class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3"
                  >

                    <div
                      class="min-w-0"
                    >

                      <h3
                        class="truncate text-base font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                      >
                        {{ vehicle.make }}
                        {{ vehicle.model }}
                      </h3>

                      <p
                        class="mt-1 text-[10px] text-zinc-500"
                      >
                        {{ vehicle.manufacturingYear }}
                        Â·

                      </p>

                    </div>


                    @if (
                      vehicle.ready
                    ) {

                      <span
                        class="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-green-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[9px] font-semibold text-green-700"
                      >
                        <span
                          class="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-green-500"
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>

                        Ready
                      </span>

                    } @else {

                      <span
                        class="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[9px] font-semibold text-amber-700"
                      >
                        <span
                          class="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500"
                          aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>

                        Needs Attention
                      </span>
                    }

                    class="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-5 gap-y-4"

                      >
                        Registration
                      </dt>

                      <dd
                        class="mt-1 truncate text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                      >
                        {{ vehicle.registrationNumber }}
                      </dd>
                    </div>


                    <div>
                      <dt
                        class="text-[8px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-zinc-400"
                      >

                        {{ vehicle.vehicleAge }}
                        years

                      <dt
                        class="text-[8px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-zinc-400"
                      >
                        Vehicle Value
                      </dt>

                      <dd
                        class="mt-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
                      >
                        â‚¹{{
                          vehicle.vehicleValue
                            | number: '1.0-0'
                        }}
                      </dd>
                    </div>


                    <div>
                      <dt
                        class="text-[8px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-zinc-400"
                      >
                        Annual Mileage
                      </dt>

                            | number

                  @if (

                  >
                    View Vehicle
                  </button>

                </div>

              </article>

            }

          </div>
        }

      </section>
    }


    <!-- ======================================================= -->
    <!-- DOWNSTREAM WORK -->
    <!-- ======================================================= -->

    <section
      class="mt-8 border-t border-[var(--color-border)] pt-7"
    >

      <div>
        <p
          class="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--color-primary)]"
        >
          Customer Operations
        </p>

        <h2
          class="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.025em] text-[var(--color-ink)]"

          Insurance work

        >
          Continue into Quotes, Policies, or Claims using
          the current Customer context.
        </p>
      </div>


      <div
        class="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3"
      >

        <!-- Quotes -->

        <button

          (click)="openQuotes()"
          class="group rounded-2xl border border-[var(--color-border)] bg-white p-5 text-left shadow-sm transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-blue-200 hover:shadow-md"
        >

          <div

          >

            >
              â†’
            </span>

          </div>

          <h3
            class="mt-5 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--color-ink)]"
          >
            Quotes

          >
            Start or continue a Customer Quote.

        <!-- Policies -->

        <button
          type="button"
          (click)="openPolicies()"
          class="group rounded-2xl border border-[var(--color-border)] bg-white p-5 text-left shadow-sm transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-blue-200 hover:shadow-md"
        >

          <div
            class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4"

FILE: customer-vehicle\pages\agent-customer-workspace\agent-customer-workspace.ts
